425	```
426	
427	| Flag | Description |
428	|------|-------------|
429	| `--symlink-dir <PATH>` | Override symlink parent [default: ~/.claude/projects] |
430	| `-l, --list` | List contents of symlinked directory after creation |
431	| `-y, --yes` | Replace existing symlink without prompting |
432	
433	### sync
434	
435	Fetch and sync a set of repos to their remotes in a single command.
436	Repo set defaults to the dual-repo workspace pair (`.` and
437	`.claude`); narrow it with `-s` / `--scope`, or point at a different
438	workspace root or single repo with `-R` / `--repo`. Check-only by
439	default — re-run with `--no-check` to apply.
440	
441	Per repo, `sync` classifies the local bookmark against its remote:
442	
443	| State | Meaning | Action on `--no-check` |
444	|------|---------|--------------------------|
445	| up-to-date | local == remote | none |
446	| behind | local is ancestor of remote | `jj bookmark set <b> -r <b>@<remote>` |
447	| ahead | remote is ancestor of local | none (push is a separate step) |
448	| diverged | neither is ancestor | `jj rebase -b <local-head> -d <b>@<remote>` |
449	| no remote | bookmark has no `@<remote>` counterpart | none — skip |
450	
451	After the bookmark action above, and only under `--no-check`, `sync`
452	repositions `@` onto the freshly-synced bookmark as a final pass — run
453	after every repo syncs cleanly, and *outside* the failure-revert
454	region below, so a reposition problem never rolls back a good
455	fetch/fast-forward. The rule differs by repo (`@-` is the parent of
456	`@`; `<b>` is the synced `--bookmark`):
457	
458	- **Code repo** (the app / workspace root):
459	  - `@` is clean (empty) and `<b>` sits ahead of `@-` on the same
460	    line → `jj new <b>` starts a fresh `@` on the new tip (the old
461	    empty `@` is auto-abandoned).
462	  - `@` has changes → `sync` asks before moving it; pass `--rebase`
463	    to answer yes up front (`jj rebase -b @ -d <b>`). Declined — or
464	    not a TTY and no `--rebase` — leaves `@` in place and says so.
465	  - `@` already sits on `<b>`, or `<b>` isn't on `@-`'s line
466	    (diverged / `@` ahead) → `@` is left untouched, with a note why.
467	- **Session repo** (`.claude`): always `jj new main` when `@-` is on
468	  `main`, so a fresh empty `@` starts each session on the tip; the
469	  prior `@` (e.g. `/exit`'s trailing session writes) is preserved as
470	  a sibling head. If `@-` isn't on `main`, `sync` errors rather than
471	  strand it.
472	
473	On any failure during fetch/classify/act — conflicted rebase,
474	subprocess error, anything — `sync` restores every repo to its
475	starting state via `jj op restore`. Either every repo advances or
476	none do. Working-copy files are preserved across the revert: jj
477	rewinds the operation log but leaves disk content untouched, and any
478	conflicted commits introduced by the failed rebase are abandoned on
479	the way back. The `@`-reposition pass runs *after* this region, so a
480	reposition error is surfaced without undoing the successful sync.
481	
482	```
483	vc-x1 sync                            # workspace-default scope, --check
484	vc-x1 sync --check                    # explicit form of the default
485	vc-x1 sync --no-check                 # workspace-default scope, apply
486	vc-x1 sync --no-check --rebase        # apply; rebase a dirty @ onto the bookmark without asking
487	vc-x1 sync --scope=code               # only the app repo
488	vc-x1 sync --scope=bot                # only the bot repo
489	vc-x1 sync --scope=code,bot           # both (explicit form of the dual default)
490	vc-x1 sync -R ../other                # sync ../other as a single repo
491	vc-x1 sync -R ../other --scope=code,bot   # ../other as workspace root
492	```
493	
494	Scripts and automation should pass `--check` or `--no-check`
495	explicitly rather than rely on the default — defaults can shift,
496	explicit flags lock in the contract. Interactive use can take the
497	default.
498	
499	**Repo set resolution.** `-R` and `--scope` compose:
500	
501	1. Neither — workspace-default scope: `code,bot` if
502	   `[workspace] other-repo` is non-empty, else `code`. POR (no
503	   `.vc-config.toml`) → `code` resolved to cwd.
504	2. `-R PATH` alone — sync just the repo at `PATH`.
505	3. `--scope=code|bot|code,bot` alone — workspace roles, resolved
506	   via the discovered workspace root's `.vc-config.toml`
507	   (`code` → root, `bot` → `root.join(other-repo)`).
508	4. `-R PATH --scope=ROLES` — roles resolved against `PATH` as the
509	   workspace root.
510	
511	Scope is cwd-portable: from `.claude/`, `vc-x1 sync` walks up to
512	the workspace root and resolves repos by absolute path.
513	
514	| Flag | Description |
515	|------|-------------|
516	| `-R, --repo <PATH>` | Workspace root, or a single repo to sync alone. Composes with `--scope` |
517	| `--scope <SCOPE>` | `code|bot|code,bot` — workspace roles to sync. Composes with `-R` |
518	| `--check` | Verify only — fetch + classify, error if any repo needs action (default) |
519	| `--no-check` | Apply — fetch + classify, then rebase/fast-forward as needed |
520	| `-q, --quiet` | Suppress all output; exit code signals result (for scripts) |
521	| `--bookmark <NAME>` | Bookmark to sync in each repo [default: main] |
522	| `--remote <NAME>` | Remote to sync against [default: origin] |
523	| `--rebase` | Rebase a non-empty `@` onto the synced bookmark without prompting (code repo only) |
524	
525	**Output shape.** Sync collapses output based on what it finds:
526	
527	- **All up-to-date** — one-line summary:
528	  `sync: N repos, all bookmarks up-to-date`. Nothing else.
529	  Makes "sprinkle sync everywhere" genuinely cheap. Scope is
530	  bookmark-vs-remote tracking — `@` may have uncommitted
531	  working-copy changes; sync intentionally doesn't speak to
532	  that (use `jj st` for working-copy state).
533	- **Action needed** (`behind` / `diverged`) — per-repo fetch +
534	  state lines, then **fatal in `--check` mode** with
535	  `sync: N repos need action — resolve with vc-x1 sync --no-check
536	  and re-run`. Under `--no-check`, the actions run instead.
537	- **`--quiet`** — no output at any level; exit code is the only
538	  signal. Intended for scripts that just need success/failure.
539	
540	**Note on the `behind` case.** jj's `git fetch` already fast-forwards a
541	tracked local bookmark when it's a strict ancestor of the incoming
542	remote, so in the common case `sync` reports `up-to-date` rather than
543	`behind`. The `behind` branch covers untracked bookmarks and edge
544	configs where auto-advance is disabled.
545	
546	### finalize
547	
548	Atomically squash, set bookmark, and push a jj repo. The primary use
549	case is the bot finalizing its own session repo (`.claude`) at the end
550	of a session. The bot can't just `jj commit` because the act of
551	committing generates more session data — files written after the commit
552	would be lost. `finalize` solves this by:
553	
554	1. **`--detach`**: spawning a background process so the bot session can
```

The `behind` branch covers untracked bookmarks and edge\nconfigs where auto-advance is disabled.\n\n### revert\n\nRestore repos to their persisted pre-sync snapshots — the explicit\nundo completing sync's stop-on-error contract. A failed\n`vc-x1 sync` leaves each repo's pre-sync `jj op` id in\n`<repo>/.vc-x1/sync-state.toml`; after inspecting what happened,\n`vc-x1 revert` runs `jj op restore <op>` in every repo holding a\nsnapshot and clears the consumed state files. Working-copy files\nare preserved across the restore: jj rewinds the operation log but\nleaves disk content untouched.\n\nRepo set resolution is identical to sync's (`-R` / `--scope` /\nworkspace default), so a failed sync and the following revert name\nthe same repos when invoked the same way. Repos without a snapshot\nare skipped with a note — sync clears state on success, so that's\nthe normal condition, not an error; finding no snapshot anywhere\nerrors (`nothing to revert`).\n\n```\nvc-x1 sync            # fails — stops, names each repo's pre-sync op id\n# ...inspect with jj st / jj log / jj op log...\nvc-x1 revert          # restore every repo to its pre-sync snapshot\n```\n\n| Flag | Description |\n|------|-------------|\n| `-R, --repo <PATH>` | Workspace root, or a single repo to revert alone. Composes with `--scope` |\n| `--scope <SCOPE>` | `code|bot|code,bot` — workspace roles to revert. Composes with `-R` |\n\n### finalize"
}
```
